## Scientific And Domain Assessments

### Identity and boundary

identity: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY.

Exact MetaCyc RIBOKIN-PWY, not a single kinase reaction or a PathBank collage. Two native enzymatic members plus the narrative-required exact spontaneous event remain. Incoming uptake and two opaque downstream interfaces preserve boundary context. Fucose mutarotase chemistry, methylglyoxal-overproduction phenotype, chemotaxis, and the historical PathBank attached lysine interface do not enlarge the core. This complete scientific assessment is inherited after fresh byte-exact maintained adoption and original-source checks.

### Chemistry and orientation

graph: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY.

Original authority structures separate beta-pyranose, beta-furanose, alpha-furanose, generic furanose and aldehydo ribose. All four equations balance atoms and charge. Native spontaneous alpha-left/beta-right reversible reaction is correctly used right-to-left. Native kinase formal generic sides and Rhea13698 remain distinct from the explicitly declared alpha pathway refinement. This complete scientific assessment is inherited after fresh byte-exact maintained adoption and original-source checks.

### Transport

completeness: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY.

Native uptake moves generic D-ribopyranose from outside to inside with all seven ATP-hydrolysis sides. Rhea29904 uses furanose and is not an exact alias. RbsB beta-pyranose binding supports presumed beta transport only, not an unqualified beta-exclusive transport equation. This complete scientific assessment is inherited after fresh byte-exact maintained adoption and original-source checks.

### Enzymes and assemblies

representation: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY.

Native RbsD10/FucU10/RbsK2 and A1C2B1 component counts are exact original slots. They are not direct assertions about Ryu tagged/untagged chromatographic mixtures. RbsA contains tandem ABC domains in one chain; frozen PathBank A2C2B1 conflict is retained. Transport-cycle states are conditional. This complete scientific assessment is inherited after fresh byte-exact maintained adoption and original-source checks.

### Cofactors and locations

evidence: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY.

Native kinase Mg2+/K+ and reviewed alternative Mn2+/NH4+ roles agree. Probable metal-nucleotide substrate and likely potassium/magnesium in vivo are not measurements of intracellular occupancy. Pi activation is separate from phosphate incorporation; no invented arsenate/vanadate identities. Localization annotations preserve source or curator inference; construct topology is not imported as native protein measurement. This complete scientific assessment is inherited after fresh byte-exact maintained adoption and original-source checks.

### Gene regulation

evidence: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY.

Native promoter regulation, full TU00206 gene membership and six exact MG1655 NCBI gene mappings agree. RbsR and CRP-cAMP target transcription. Native YidZ RNA-seq and Gao primary rbsD deletion response support a negative sign under stated M9-glucose conditions; tagged ChIP-exo and supplement limits remain explicit. Unnumbered RbsR-ribose and CRP-cAMP ligand slots do not imply counts. This complete scientific assessment is inherited after fresh byte-exact maintained adoption and original-source checks.

provenance: supported. Targets: MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Complete scientific acceptance covers this exact source-defined route and explicit refinements, not experimental demonstration of every alternative and regulatory condition simultaneously in wild-type MG1655.
- Ryu 2004 full scientific text was observed through an author upload; publisher PDF and supplementary figures were not obtained. The finalizer inherits that reading after exact source-byte checks. Other primary access is explicitly limited to the sections inspected by the independent scientist, plus the finalizer selected Gao passages.
- Native complex assemblies are not equated with tagged/untagged gel-filtration estimates. RbsC topology comes from His-tagged mutant preparations; YidZ RNA-seq and 8-myc ChIP-exo conditions remain distinct.
- The kinase alpha-specific equation is a declared pathway-narrative refinement of generic formal native/Rhea sides. Generic D-ribopyranose uptake is not a beta-exclusive transport claim or an exact furanose Rhea alias. Opaque downstream interfaces do not assert cyclic/open-chain endpoint identity.
- Database expression/inference annotations remain source-limited; iModulon membership stays computational context and establishes no causal edge.
- Complete accepted prior science, authority builds and implementation successor review are inherited only after exact current-byte comparisons. This finalizer does not claim to repeat full corpus gates, full native builds, whole original Rhea streaming, all primary-paper readings or Semantic SQL body hashing.
- Licensing remains deferred under the authorized task. No source-exhaustion or corpus-wide scientific re-review conclusion is made.
- External author-audit findings and issue 436 are retained through evidence-linked assessments. They are not fabricated canonical predecessors, and saving this review does not close issues, merge or publish.
- The test suite records three skipped tests and three dependency warnings. Its -q output does not identify the skipped cases; this review does not infer them or claim they ran.
- Visual QA is static light-theme SVG/PNG and anchor/triple verification. No new interactive browser, keyboard, zoom, mobile or dark-theme test is claimed. Dense overview labels require the native full-size graph and evidence table.
- Complete staged whitespace, commit/PR review, issue closure, merge and publication are later root-owned lifecycle steps and are not claimed here. Canonical validate/save/check follow assembly; their actual receipts are recorded separately rather than predeclared as successful inside their own immutable review.
- Initial native inspect preceded maintained assessment. Final inspect followed actual completed root gates b9d97d/0, preservation ba6761/0 and archive a6bf4d/0. All initial input hashes remained unchanged; actual source-reading scope and inherited work remain explicit.
- RIBOSE-AUDIT-01 through -05 and issue 436 are external review history with source-backed accepted corrections. No previous_occurrences is fabricated for them.
- The retained external implementation packages preserve earlier rejected controls, setup/lint failures and the accepted corrected successor. A hash match is used for inheritance, not as a substitute for the prior scientific review.

scope:
  description: 'One entire maintained record: three core chemical events, incoming
    transport, alternative enzymes/assemblies, cofactor/location/regulatory context
    and two opaque downstream pathways. All 66 edges/107 evidence items/51 references/51
    named IDs covered through byte-exact inherited independent scientific review plus
    fresh maintained verification and scoped source inspection.'
  selection: Exact MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY at SHA-256 385101d166c9150936d81bb0f1c6c0d134b6ac1e6a6454d86e238ac0576e3d71;
    no sampling or route reduction.
  coverage: full
  population_size: 1
  reviewed_target_ids:
  - MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY
  exclusions:
  - target: Other 226 maintained records
    reason: Exact deterministic preservation and full corpus gates are covered; no
      fresh scientific rereview of every older pathway.
  - target: Complete downstream NONOXIPENT-PWY and PWY0-662 chemistry
    reason: Only exact source-supported opaque interfaces are asserted, without cyclic/open-chain
      equivalence or expanded downstream reactions.
targets:
- target_id: MetaCyc:RIBOKIN-PWY
  path: data/pathways/ribose-phosphorylation.yaml
  label: ribose phosphorylation
  kind: maintained
  owner_paths:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/PathwayMech
    path: data/pathways/ribose-phosphorylation.yaml
    role: Maintained pathway identity, chemical scope, components and edge evidence
      owner

findings: []
actions: []
limitations:
- Complete scientific acceptance covers this exact source-defined route and explicit
  refinements, not experimental demonstration of every alternative and regulatory
  condition simultaneously in wild-type MG1655.
- Ryu 2004 full scientific text was observed through an author upload; publisher PDF
  and supplementary figures were not obtained. The finalizer inherits that reading
  after exact source-byte checks. Other primary access is explicitly limited to the
  sections inspected by the independent scientist, plus the finalizer selected Gao
  passages.
- Native complex assemblies are not equated with tagged/untagged gel-filtration estimates.
  RbsC topology comes from His-tagged mutant preparations; YidZ RNA-seq and 8-myc
  ChIP-exo conditions remain distinct.
- The kinase alpha-specific equation is a declared pathway-narrative refinement of
  generic formal native/Rhea sides. Generic D-ribopyranose uptake is not a beta-exclusive
  transport claim or an exact furanose Rhea alias. Opaque downstream interfaces do
  not assert cyclic/open-chain endpoint identity.
- Database expression/inference annotations remain source-limited; iModulon membership
  stays computational context and establishes no causal edge.
- Complete accepted prior science, authority builds and implementation successor review
  are inherited only after exact current-byte comparisons. This finalizer does not
  claim to repeat full corpus gates, full native builds, whole original Rhea streaming,
  all primary-paper readings or Semantic SQL body hashing.
- Licensing remains deferred under the authorized task. No source-exhaustion or corpus-wide
  scientific re-review conclusion is made.
- External author-audit findings and issue 436 are retained through evidence-linked
  assessments. They are not fabricated canonical predecessors, and saving this review
  does not close issues, merge or publish.
- The test suite records three skipped tests and three dependency warnings. Its -q
  output does not identify the skipped cases; this review does not infer them or claim
  they ran.
- Visual QA is static light-theme SVG/PNG and anchor/triple verification. No new interactive
  browser, keyboard, zoom, mobile or dark-theme test is claimed. Dense overview labels
  require the native full-size graph and evidence table.
- Complete staged whitespace, commit/PR review, issue closure, merge and publication
  are later root-owned lifecycle steps and are not claimed here. Canonical validate/save/check
  follow assembly; their actual receipts are recorded separately rather than predeclared
  as successful inside their own immutable review.
links:
- https://github.com/CultureBotAI/PathwayMech/issues/436
notes:
- Initial native inspect preceded maintained assessment. Final inspect followed actual
  completed root gates b9d97d/0, preservation ba6761/0 and archive a6bf4d/0. All initial
  input hashes remained unchanged; actual source-reading scope and inherited work
  remain explicit.
- RIBOSE-AUDIT-01 through -05 and issue 436 are external review history with source-backed
  accepted corrections. No previous_occurrences is fabricated for them.
- The retained external implementation packages preserve earlier rejected controls,
  setup/lint failures and the accepted corrected successor. A hash match is used for
  inheritance, not as a substitute for the prior scientific review.
